S-0609 (revision 5; built at revision 3, revision 4 taken in at M7a step 1c, [docs/m7-claims/step1c-m7a.md](../m7-claims/step1c-m7a.md), revision 5 at step 1d, [docs/m7-claims/step1d-m7a.md](../m7-claims/step1d-m7a.md)) is built in `glasnost_sim::gattling`; the ledger is [docs/m7-claims/S-0609.md](../m7-claims/S-0609.md), the tests are `claims_s0609.rs`, the audio test `spin_sound_draws` and, for the importer, `gattling_spin_settings`. The state is the spin value and gattling stage (`CombatState::spin`, `gattling_stage`), the animation counter (`CombatState::anim_frame`) and the elapsed-tick count (`Behaviours::elapsed`); all are saved and hashed. Save format version 48.

- **Spin-up and spin-down (claims 1 to 4, F1 to F3).** `gattling::spin_up` and `spin_down` apply F1 and F2 with the normal or, while the object is elite, the elite thresholds (an absent elite threshold is 0). The rise test reads the value before the update; there is no clamp. `quirks.gattling_zero_spin_down_empties` (a spin-down of amount 0 empties the value) is wired and tested in both positions.
- **Vehicles (claims 5, 6, 12).** `combat::behave` calls `gattling::vehicle_step` once per firing step with the firing check's answer for slot 0 (no target or an empty slot 0 spins down; the deploy-to-fire exit makes no update). A passenger or an object being erased takes no firing step, so its spin is frozen.
- **Buildings (claims 7 to 9, F4, F5).** `process` counts the elapsed ticks of a gattling building (`gattling::elapse`); its attack processing calls `building_attack` with the answer and multiplier c, a SAM building makes no update, and guard, sticky and area guard processings call `building_guard` (spin-down by c). `gattling::building_turn` runs in the building's own turn after its processing (`tick.rs`): the passive spin-down outside attack more than G + 5 ticks after the last shot, and the animation counter. **Buildings now import `TurretCount`** (S-0308 Import mapping, type section; `BUILDING_FIELDS`), so a gattling building is a multi-weapon type that chooses its weapon by stage.
- **Resets (claim 11).** Boarding resets value, stage and elapsed count (`transport::reset_gattling_spin`, which was a seam); each successful temporal lock, a joining one included, gives one spin-down (`temporal`). Entering an absorbing building resets too (S-0605 claim 7, built at M7a step 3.5).
- **Sound (claim 13).** The simulation reports each update as `Effect::Spin` (`SpinEvent::Up { raised, weapon }` with the slot-2s′ weapon after elite replacement, `Down`, `Left` when the object leaves the map) and marks a gattling firer's shots so they play no per-shot report. The audio crate keeps the playing mark and takes the draws on the unsynchronised stream (`Audio::spin`); an unresolved report list counts as empty. Nothing of this reaches game state.
- **Animation (claim 14).** `anim_frame` advances for a vehicle once per completed firing step with value at least 1, for a building once per tick with positive value after its processing. The original's drawing-only double advance outside attack is not reproduced. The client reads it for a building's turret frame and a vehicle's (`render::voxel::vehicle_turret_frame`).
- **Importer.** `WeaponStages`, `RateUp`, `RateDown`, `Stage1` to `Stage6` and `EliteStage1` to `EliteStage6`, read per layer while the gattling flag is set and the count is at least 2 (`sim_combat::spin_settings`); warnings for a count above 6, negative settings, a final count below 2 after an earlier layer set the thresholds (at the layer that reduced it; a count raised back to 2 or more gives no warning) and empty stage slots 2s.
- **Settings outside the domain (revision 4, answering Q-1220; revision 5, answering Q-1151).** Engine rules with no quirk switch: negative stage counts, amounts and thresholds are used as 0, with an importer warning for each; spin products, sums and the elapsed-tick count saturate at 2³¹ − 1 (`saturation_at_the_32_bit_bound`); and the stage count as the layers finally leave it governs, so a type a later layer lowered below 2 stages uses no thresholds (all 0, though the importer still writes the held rows: the cap is 0, the value never grows, the stage stays 0; `layered_reduction_below_2_uses_no_thresholds`), and the importer warns about the reducing layer (`gattling_spin_settings`).
- **Client and lane.** The local lane scene `gattling` (`crates/app/tests/m7a_lane.rs`, screenshots `local-lane/m7a/gattling-*.png`): a gattling vehicle ordered at an enemy vehicle climbs to its top stage (stage 1 after 200 ticks, stage 2 after 400) and, its target removed, falls back to 0 within 23 ticks; a gattling building beside an enemy vehicle climbs to its top stage through guard and attack. AI lane (map 8, seed 7, Yuri house 1, after merging M7a step 1b): sides 0,2: Allied won at 15 708; the Yuri house had 2 gattling types, ordered 2 gattling defences, kept 12 spins, never had a gattling at its top stage, built one controller and took nothing. Sides 1,2: Soviet won at 13 301; 2 gattling types, 1 defence ordered, 210 spins kept, 218 house-ticks at the top stage, 1 takeover. Replays and mid-game saves matched in both.

Seams: none (the absorption reset is built with S-0605 at M7a step 3.5). Interim rules waiting for answers: none (Q-1220 is answered by revision 4, Q-1151 by revision 5).

## Simulation (M7a: gas clouds)

S-0613 (revision 5 since M7a step 1e, [step1e-m7a.md](../m7-claims/step1e-m7a.md); built at revision 4; S-0202 revision 29, S-0010 revision 18, S-0109 revision 29 and S-0203 revision 36, which restate its F9, its loading-time Edge case and the shared system's place, were taken in at step 1f with no behaviour change, [step1f-m7a.md](../m7-claims/step1f-m7a.md)) is built in `glasnost_sim::gas`; the ledger is [docs/m7-claims/S-0613.md](../m7-claims/S-0613.md), the tests are `claims_s0613.rs`, the unit tests in `gas.rs` and, for the importer, `sim_gas_tests.rs`. The state is `World::gas` (`GasWorld`: the particle systems with their particles, the shared system's object number and the virus-death animations); all of it is saved and hashed. Save format version 49.

- **Particle systems as world objects (claims 1, 4, 6, 17).** A particle system takes an object number and a place in the update order; `tick::object_turn` runs `gas::system_turn` for systems and `gas::anim_turn` for virus-death animations, after projectiles. The shared gas system (`GasCloudSys`) is the last object of the game-start step to enter play (`load::new_game`, just before the ore-twinkle setup): after the map-placed objects, the starting units, the crates and every tile or cell animation of loading, so it is appended to the update order after all of them and takes the object number after them (map objects start at 1; since step 1e, in a game with a shared system, every object created in play has a number one higher than before, and so does every particle, which flips F8's parity for them). A particle requested while loading (a μ = 0 tile or cell animation) meets no shared system: its creation draws are taken and no particle is made (Edge cases, a departure; the importer warns for a cell animation, theater tile animations are not seen by the rules import, a seam). A warhead naming a particle system creates it at the end of its detonation (`combat::area::detonate`, S-0303 claim 20) with its first particle. A system of every kind is kept and takes its turns (claim 4); since step 1h the smoke, fire, spark and railgun kinds keep their first particle and run their own turns (S-0616, section below; Q-1243 answered). After the kind's turn work, every system's lifetime goes down by 1 whatever its value (−1 goes on to −2 and below), it is marked to end when the result is exactly 0, and a marked empty system leaves play in the same turn (claim 17). A shared system that leaves play is not created again: later requests take their draws and make no particle (a departure; the importer warns when its lifetime is positive). The gate and lane cloud counters count gas-kind systems only (`gas::is_cloud`).
- **Virus death (claims 2, 3).** `combat::death::infantry_death` calls `gas::virus_death` for row 8 of S-0107 claim 17 and for the extra cases (a not-human type, or a parachuting unit hit by a kind-8 warhead). The animation is a narrow one-pass playback (`VirusAnim`) whose middle event puts its particles into the shared system; the extra cases deliver their middle event at once and one more particle. S-0109's middle event (`load::middle_event`) now delivers its particles into the shared system too.
- **Particles (claims 5 to 15, F1 to F8).** Creation with the S-0202 creation draws (E = (E_max + u) mod 65536, F9); the update pass newest first (on even ticks drift, vertical speed and deflection, then damage, stage and lifetime, claim 18), then the second pass (moves, removals, successors newest dead first). F3's deflection runs in soft single and double precision (`glasnost_num`) through the S-0106 table C4 ramp matrices and is stored as bits. Gas hits go through `combat::damage::apply` with no attacker and the system's house (a berserk warhead therefore works, S-0610's seam) and report `Effect::GasHit`. `quirks.wind_southeast_short` is wired.
- **Importer.** Particle fields `MaxDC`, `Damage`, `Warhead`, `StartFrame`, `StartStateAI`, `EndStateAI`, `StateAIAdvance`, `DeleteOnStateLimit`, `NextParticle`, `NextParticleOffset`, `WindEffect`, `Translucency`; particle-system `BehavesLike`, `HoldsWhat` (absent from a section that is read: the empty-named type, which `layers.rs` registers by reference; a section never read: none; S-0613 revision 6, step 1g), `Lifetime`; General `InfantryVirus`, `WindDirection`. Warnings for a virus animation without a particle type, a wind effect above 10, a wind direction outside 0 to 7 when a type has a wind effect, stage advances of 0 or −1, a shared gas system with a positive lifetime (claim 17), and an overlay cell animation with μ = 0 that requests particles while loading (Edge cases; only when the art lookup ran, since μ needs it).
- **Client.** Every gas particle is drawn as a green placeholder cloud (`Marker::Gas`); claim 16's art, translucency and frame-rate cutoff are not drawn yet. The live view counts gas hits and the most particles at once. Local lane figures from step 3.4, not re-run at step 1e (particles now live longer, F9): scene `gas` (`crates/app/tests/m7a_lane.rs`, screenshots `local-lane/m7a/gas-*.png`): a virus unit's kill leaves the virus animation and up to 3 clouds at once, which deal 2 hits; the install's virus warhead names no particle system. AI lane (map 8, seed 7, Yuri house 1): sides 0,2: Allied won at 16 254; the Yuri house had 1 virus unit; 3 clouds, 25 gas hits, at most 6 particles. Sides 1,2: Soviet won at 13 301 (as before); 1 virus unit, no clouds. Replays and mid-game saves matched in both.
- **Gate.** The duel's gattling top-stage weapon fires a kind-8 warhead naming a cloud system, with a virus animation and the shared system in the fixture; the run counts gas clouds and gas hits (step 3.4: seed 2 had 184 clouds and 274 hits; over seeds 1 to 16 the gas minima were 16 clouds and 26 hits). The change moved some earlier minima (old to new): units made berserk 20 to 25, intent ticks 1 982 to 1 942, slaves at work 6 312 to 6 812, stage rises 9 to 2 (seed 6), top-stage ticks 1 396 to 331, takeover orders 6 to 5, takeovers 10 to 10. A buildable virus infantry type instead lowered more of them (it takes a share of the barracks). At step 1e (revision 5: the shared system's new place, E = E_max + u, the count-down of every lifetime) only `duel-m7a.txt` moved; the goldens were regenerated. Over seeds 1 to 16 the gas minima are now 32 clouds and 141 hits (medians 182 and 717; the clouds live longer with F9). Other minima (step 3.4 to step 1e): units made berserk 25 to 19, intent ticks 1 942 to 1 776, slaves at work 6 812 to 5 537, stage rises 2 to 4, top-stage ticks 331 to 711, takeover orders 5 to 7, takeovers 10 to 8; psychic sensor orders stay 2 in every seed (the known exception).

Seams: claim 16 (drawing detail); laser-fence sections in F3's obstacle test count as inactive; the importer's loading-time warning for theater tile animations. Revisions 7 and 8 (step 1h): owner removal marks a system of every kind, and the shared system stands at S-0616's constant (2688, 2688, 0). Q-1153 and Q-1243 are answered by S-0616 (step 1h). Revision 9 (step 1j): claim 7's one successor is made for a dead particle of any kind in a gas system, the system's kind deciding (S-0616 claim 3; Q-1155 answered). Revision 10 (step 1j): that successor takes the dead particle's speed as well as its velocity, and nothing else (`gas::second_pass`). Q-1240, Q-1241, Q-1242 and Q-1244 are answered by revision 5 (step 1e).

## Simulation (M7a: particle systems)

S-0616 (revision 4, taken in at M7a step 1h, [step1h-m7a.md](../m7-claims/step1h-m7a.md)) is built in `glasnost_sim::particles`, only as far as built specs need it; the ledger is [docs/m7-claims/S-0616.md](../m7-claims/S-0616.md), the tests are `claims_s0616.rs`, the unit tests in `particles.rs`, the creator tests in `claims_s0302.rs`, `claims_s0601.rs` and `claims_s0614.rs` and, for the importer, `particle_system_kind_fields`, `particle_system_kind_warnings`, `default_spark_system_absent_warns` and `particle_quirks_load_in_both_positions`. The systems live with the gas systems in `World::gas`; `gas::system_turn` dispatches on the system's kind and each particle updates and moves by its own kind (claim 3). The kept state (speed, flight direction, spark velocity, fire step, emission timer, spark counter, own stage-advance value, target point, owner object) is saved and hashed. Save format version 52 (54 since step 1j, for the depot memory of S-0314 revision 25 and the fate tick of S-0605 revision 7). Revision 5 (step 1i) and revision 6 (step 1j, [step1j-m7a.md](../m7-claims/step1j-m7a.md)) are taken in: since revision 6 successors follow the system's kind, not the particle's (claim 3; Q-1155 answered): one successor in a gas system (S-0613 claim 7, revision 9), claim 8's two in a smoke system, none in a fire, spark or railgun system, for a dead particle of any kind (`claim_3_successors_follow_the_system_kind`). Revision 7 (step 1j): a gas system's successor takes the dead particle's speed and velocity (S-0613 claim 7, revision 10).

- **Creators built (claim 1).** A warhead's system of any kind keeps its first particle (`gas::warhead_system`). The damage smoke of S-0302 claim 13 is a smoke system owned by the damaged object at its position plus the type's damage-smoke displacement (`particles::damage_smoke`, `Object::combat.damage_smoke`); a hit above the yellow ratio marks it and the object keeps it until it leaves play (Q-1153 answered). The overload sparks of S-0601 claim 16 and the drone delivery sparks of S-0614 claim 9 are systems of the game-wide spark type (`particles::spark_system`; none without `[CombatDamage] DefaultSparkSystem`, which the importer warns about).
- **Owner removal (claim 4).** `objects::remove` calls `particles::owner_removed`: every system the object owns loses it and is marked.
- **Kinds (claims 5 to 19, F1 to F4, F8 to F13).** Smoke: update newest first, removal pass with successors (claim 8, F3), move with the smoke wind table and the deck stop (F2), emission and the timer's cut-off (claim 9, F4). Fire: update and step (F8), move, hits on the objects of the cell (F9) with no attacking house. Spark: the burst (F10) and the motion and contact with double gravity (F11). Railgun: the line (F12) once, then marked. The normalised stage-advance value (F13) is formed at creation. Every listed draw is taken, the drawn-only ones included. Single and double precision run in `glasnost_num`.
- **Quirks.** `fire_particle_no_house`, `smoke_ignores_particle_cap`, `spark_double_gravity`, `smoke_deck_extra_update` (each tested both ways) and `flame_sweep_facing` (at M7a a seam that changed nothing; wired at M7b step 3.1, below).

Seams at M7a (all but `electric_bolt_sparks` and `voxel_animation_system` filled at M7b step 3.1, see *Simulation (M7b: particle systems)*; those two at M8 step C5 parts 1 and 2): `effect_weapon_emitter` (fire, spark and railgun emitters of effect weapons, the let-go events, S-0301 claim 5's live-effect gate, the flame sweep F5, the swing F6, the insertion F7, a system's target object), `barrel_particle_system` (S-0303 claim 19 takes its draw only), `destruction_smoke`, `refinery_smoke`, `natural_smoke` (with S-0516's cloak removal and uncloak rule), `electric_bolt_sparks`, `damage_sparks`, `deactivated_sparks`, `voxel_animation_system`, the trigger actions (S-0011 codes 88 and 89), and the drawing of every kind. No object is inside a tunnel, so claim 9's tunnel test always passes. No interim rule is waiting (Q-1155 answered by revision 6, step 1j). Gate (step 1h, after merging main with S-0603, seeds 1 to 16): every minimum and median is the same as on main before the merge (berserk orders 9/19, units made berserk 27/47, sensor orders 2/2, the known exception, intent ticks 1 869/3 316, reveal orders 6/11, reveals fired 6/11, slave-miner buildings 3/5, slave ticks 1 274/7 193, gattling stage rises 6/14, top-stage ticks 448/2 373, takeover orders 3/14, takeovers 7/15, gas clouds 26/147, gas hits 39/487, absorber entry orders 6/15, infantry absorbed 5/8, grinder entry orders 4/8, units ground 4/8); the duel's seed stays 3 (12 000 ticks). The goldens were regenerated.

## Simulation (M7a: Infantry absorber)

S-0605 (revision 2 since step 1g) is built in `glasnost_sim::structures::absorb`; the ledger is [docs/m7-claims/S-0605.md](../m7-claims/S-0605.md), the tests are `claims_s0605.rs`, two pairs in `pairs_m7a.rs` (a controlled enemy goes into an absorber and comes out an enemy; a released neutral unit takes the bio-reactor fate), the audio test `bio_reactor_sounds` and, for the importer, `absorber_fields`. The state is in `StructState`: a building's passengers in the passenger order, front (the most recent entrant) first (`absorbed`), a passenger's building (`absorbed_in`) the building an actor waits in line for (`waiting`) and the enter-nearest instruction for absorbing buildings (`enter_nearest`, claim 16); an entrant's contact is the ordinary contact field. All of it is saved and hashed. Save format version 51.

- **Acceptance and capacity (claims 1, 2, F1).** `absorb::answer` is the docking answer of S-0214 rows x3, x4, q2 and q3 for an absorbing building (`targeting` reads it, so the *enter* cursor and click follow it): alliance both ways, switched on, the right kind, not balloon-hover, not a controller holding a victim, then F1 (n + 1 ≤ c and size ≤ L, L compared in soft floating point). A building type carries passengers for S-0214 rows x and q when its capacity is above 0; row I13's "carries no passengers" is a capacity equal to 0, so a negative capacity meets neither (claim 1; since S-0214 revision 40, step 1h, for any building type, `targeting::building_passengers`).
- **Contact and waiting in line (claims 3 to 5, 17, F2).** `absorb::order_entered` runs when an *enter* order on an absorbing building is applied (`orders.rs`, after the order's ordinary effects), and the fate of claim 16 and the retry run the same rule (`seek_contact`): a building that holds any contact puts the actor in line with its destination cleared; otherwise it asks for contact (alliance both ways, health above 0, a free link place). Not granted, the actor does not wait and keeps the building as its destination; granted, it asks to load, and a refused loading (switched off) gives the contact up again, the destination kept. `absorb::waiting_turn` is the per-tick retry in the actor's own turn after its movement (`tick.rs`, where the depot retry is; nothing while phasing in): granted and accepted, *enter* queued with an immediate start and the building as destination; refused, contact given up, the none behaviour queued (no-op) and the destination cleared. Link places are max(NumberOfDocks, 1) (`absorb::link_places`). `absorb::process_enter` repeats the loading request at each *enter* processing (`process.rs`); a refusal gives up the contact and falls back to rest with the destination and walk kept, so the entrant walks on and never goes in (scenario A6). A waiting actor's first *enter* processing is the approach (`absorb::approach`): S-0212 claim 25's nearby search from the cell of the building's reference point with table T5's waiting absorber row (the step-off settings, shared as `path::NearbySettings::own_class`: own class with winged as track, own zone, the centre's reach region read for the deck, deck mode on a bridge deck, no height, further or overlay test, no reference cell, so position *N* mod *n*), *move* started at once, the waiting kept; with no cell found it falls back to rest in that processing and keeps waiting (S-0605 revision 5, Q-1152 answered at step 1i; `claim_4_approach_one_free_cell`, `claim_4_approach_tie_by_tick`, `claim_4_approach_no_cell`). Since step 1j (revision 6) a vehicle ordered in to a vehicle absorber always waits in line, even at an empty building: no destination and no contact at the order, then the approach and the retry (`absorb::seek_contact`, `claim_3_vehicle_always_waits_in_line`); since revision 7 claim 16's fate puts a vehicle victim in line the same way, and it sends no contact request in the release tick (`StructState::fate_tick`, `claim_3_vehicle_fate_waits_in_line`); setting up a wait forgets any remembered entry object (in our engine only S-0314 claim 23's depot is one), and an enter order on an absorbing building gives none. Without contact and not waiting the processing falls back at once. A contact ends only in claim 17's cases; `quirks.absorber_stale_contact_kept` (default on) keeps a contact held outside *enter* (off: given up in the holder's turn, the old Q-1267 rule), tested both ways.
- **Going in (claims 6 to 8).** `absorb::arrival` (`walk.rs`) for infantry at the end of a walking step in a cell of the building: one entry raise on the building's tag (S-0524 claim 12), unconditional; without contact nothing more; then the controlled entrant's release (S-0601 claim 9, the release sound before the entry sound), off the map, front passenger, power due when the extra output is above 0, the gattling reset (S-0609 claim 11, `gattling::reset`), the entry sound. `absorb::vehicle_arrival` (`drive.rs`, `hover.rs`) is claim 8's vehicle absorber: the full test on arrival, refused: destination cleared, *guard* queued, a forced scatter. `quirks.absorber_contact_no_capacity_recheck` (default on) is wired and tested both ways.
- **Inside and owners (claims 9, 10).** Passengers are off the map, keep their owner and are left out of the crate infantry count (`crates::infantry_count`); their power (S-0405 claim 1, `econ::power`) follows the building to a new owner.
- **Release (claims 11 to 13, F3).** The deploy click on an absorbing building with passengers gives *unload* (S-0214 row B5 and table M); `absorb::process_unload` lets out the front passenger per processing by S-0507 claim 10's exit search from the top-left cell, rear direction q = (facing + 4) mod 8, exit facing 8192 × e, then a move two cells out; no exit puts the passenger back in front and blocks the others; an empty processing queues *guard* and waits 1 tick. `quirks.absorber_release_second_round_skips_rear` and `quirks.absorber_release_bridge_ends_search` are wired and tested both ways. A voluntary release clears the enter-nearest instruction for a human-controlled owner only (claim 13).
- **Other ways out (claims 14, 15).** Destruction (`absorb::destroyed`, from `combat::death`, S-0312 claim 24: front first, one per foundation cell, the sale's centre placement with one draw per open cell (claim 14); no crew and killing damage that ignores defences kills them), sale (`absorb::sold`, S-0408 claim 8), erasure (`absorb::erased`, S-0514 claim 12: deleted), removal inside (`absorb::removing`: n goes down; `quirks.absorber_removal_keeps_stale_power`, default on, makes no power due so the totals stay stale until something else makes them due; off: power due; tested both ways).
- **Seams filled.** The bio-reactor fate of S-0601 claim 8 (`absorb::bio_reactor_fate`, from `mind::fate`, claim 16: the unit takes the enter-nearest instruction; candidates in the owner's absorber list, the house's holding order `econ.acquired`, each asked claim 1's test; the smallest ρ₃ to the reference point wins, ties to the later one in the list; found: *enter* queued without a start and the destination assigned, which applies claim 3; none: instruction cleared, *hunt*). The instruction repeats the search at every *guard* and *area guard* processing (`absorb::instruction_turn` from `process.rs`, *D*<sub>r</sub>, no draw; S-0215 claim 24 and claim 26 row 3; since S-0215 revision 60, step 1h, answering Q-1154, waiting in line for the building counts as already bound); `quirks.enter_nearest_survives_absorption` (default on) keeps it through an entry, so a neutral passenger let out walks back in (off: entry clears it), tested both ways; the gattling reset of S-0609 claim 11; the absorber entry raise of `mp_triggers`.
- **Importer.** Buildings `InfantryAbsorb`, `UnitAbsorb`, `Passengers`, `SizeLimit` (and the existing `NumberOfDocks`, `ExtraPower`); `[AudioVisual] EnterBioReactorSound` and `LeaveBioReactorSound`, played by the audio crate (`Sound::EnterBioReactor` and `Sound::LeaveBioReactor` reports).
- **Client and lane.** The local lane scene `absorber` (`crates/app/tests/m7a_lane.rs`, screenshots `local-lane/m7a/absorber-*.png`): three infantry clicked onto the install's infantry absorber get contact one at a time (one link place; the others wait in line), the first is inside after 95 ticks, all three after 322, the house's output rises; the deploy click empties it in 31 ticks and the output goes back. The building's passenger animation (S-0108 claim 17) is not drawn; its passenger pips (S-0111 claim 12) are, since M8 step A3 intake 46. AI lane (map 8, seed 7, Yuri house 1): sides 0,2: Allied won at 20 112; the Yuri house had 3 absorbing buildings, gave 4 entry orders, 1 infantry went in. Sides 1,2: Soviet won at 13 305; 2 absorbing buildings, 1 entry order, none went in. Replays and mid-game saves matched in both.
- **Gate.** Alpha's power plant in the M7a duel is BIOP, an infantry absorber (power 50, capacity 5, size limit 1, extra power 40; the fixture's POWR is Beta's only); the run counts entry orders and infantry that went in, and saves and loads at the first tick a passenger is inside. Seed 2: 34 entry orders, 10 absorbed. Minimum / median over seeds 1 to 16 (after merging main at 75350df): berserk orders 6/13, units made berserk 22/29, sensor orders 2/2 (the known exception), intent ticks 1 912/3 740, reveal orders 7/12, reveals fired 7/12, slave-miner buildings 5/8, slave ticks 2 765/8 338, gattling stage rises 4/13, top-stage ticks 868/2 279, takeover orders 7/17, takeovers 11/16, gas clouds 36/110, gas hits 144/404, absorber entry orders 12/20, infantry absorbed 3/7. Every scenario's golden moved from tick 1 (new saved state); the goldens were regenerated. At step 1g (S-0605 revision 2; save format 51, AI game format 20 with the stalled-entrant re-send) the minimum / median over seeds 1 to 16: berserk orders 6/17, units made berserk 20/28, sensor orders 2/2 (the known exception), intent ticks 1 770/3 224, reveal orders 7/12, reveals fired 7/12, slave-miner buildings 6/8, slave ticks 286/6 016, gattling stage rises 4/13, top-stage ticks 461/2 035, takeover orders 3/13, takeovers 10/16, gas clouds 14/100, gas hits 15/404, absorber entry orders 10/18, infantry absorbed 4/8. Without the re-send the infantry-absorbed minimum was 2 (seeds 11, 13 and 14: a contact kept outside *enter*, claim 17). The goldens were regenerated.

Seams: the passenger pips and animation (drawing); teams are still a seam of S-0601 claim 8 (the grinder fate is built with S-0603). Q-1260 to Q-1267 are answered by revision 2 (step 1g). Q-1154 is answered by S-0215 revision 60 (step 1h). Q-1152 is answered by S-0605 revision 5 (step 1i). Revisions 6 and 7 at step 1j. Q-1159 is answered by S-0605 revision 8 (step 1k) as the interim had it: a vehicle still driving from an earlier order is ready at its first start, because a queued *enter* lifts S-0215 claim 11's moving condition, so it makes *enter* current and takes the approach step. Save format 54 since step 1j.

## Simulation (M7a: Grinder)

S-0603 (revision 2) is built in `glasnost_sim::structures::grinder`; the ledger is [docs/m7-claims/S-0603.md](../m7-claims/S-0603.md), the tests are `claims_s0603.rs`, two pairs in `pairs_m7a.rs` (a controlled enemy is ground for its controller's house; a released neutral unit takes the grinder fate), the audio test `grinder_consumption_sounds` and, for the importer, `grinder_fields`. No state of its own: an entrant is a unit in *eaten* with the building as its destination, so save format 50 is unchanged.

- **Order and answer (claims 1, 2).** `grinder::answer` is the docking answer of S-0214 table V rows n1 and n2 and row I26 (`targeting` reads it, so the *recycle* cursor and click follow it): one-way alliance, not under construction or being sold, a link place (F2), the naval match, not a balloon, not a loaded vehicle or a controller with a victim, switched on. A building that also absorbs answers by S-0605 claim 1.
- **Approach and consumption (claims 3 to 7).** No contact and no link place; `grinder::process_eaten` is S-0215 claim 28's table for *eaten*. Infantry is consumed at the end of a walking step in a foundation cell (`walk.rs`), a vehicle at the cell-arrival update of the first foundation cell (`drive.rs`, `hover.rs`; occupancy rows VP6 and VP7). The consumption tick: the entry raise, `Effect::Consumed`, the payments, the parasite's host-consumed rule, the entrant's destroyed-by-anything raise, the silent removal of passengers (two levels) and `objects::remove`. `quirks.grinder_no_arrival_recheck` is wired and tested both ways.
- **Payments (claims 8 to 11).** Each consumed object pays the building's owner `sell::refund` of its type with its own owner's factors (a controlled entrant with the controlling house's); health and a harvester's load play no part. No kill, no loss, no `Killed` effect.
- **Interruptions (claim 15).** A new order, a destroyed or sold building and an owner change end the trip (`objects::remove` clears destinations). A path search that fails more than 1 cell from the building clears the destination and queues *guard* (S-0603 revision 4 claims 3 and 15, S-0212 claim 23 row 2; `claim_3_failed_approach_gives_up`). Since step 1k (S-0603 revision 7, S-0215 revision 63) a driving vehicle whose body is still turning counts as moving, so that *guard* waits for the turn to end (`process::moving`).
- **Seams filled.** The grinder fate of S-0601 claim 8 (`mind::grinder_fate`: the nearest own recycling building by ρ₃, ties to the latest placed, *eaten* queued); the recycling consumption of S-0524 claim 12's consuming seam with its passenger removal.
- **Seam left.** Claim 12's *active-1* / *special-1* swap (display only).
- **Own AI.** `glasnost_ai::grinder` (AI game format 21, `Uses.grinds`; 20 on this branch until step 1g took 20): the house builds one grinder, the cheapest it can place, once it holds a unit it would send (an idle controlled enemy vehicle or a badly damaged own unit), queued behind a building already on its line and paid as it goes (`Brain::build_grinder`; until step 6 it waited for a free line and twice its cost in hand, which a house short of money never has). It sends idle controlled enemy vehicles (and controlled enemy harvesters, which do no work for it; controlled infantry go to an absorber with room first), badly damaged own units (at most 30 % strength; vehicles only without a repair depot) and, while credits are below 1 000 and no absorber has room, own infantry at home beyond four. Every order is the *recycle* click; a unit more than 12 cells away is first clicked to a cell 4 cells off the grinder. The base defence leaves units bound for a grinder alone. All numbers are `nocite` tuning. Tests: `crates/deterministic/ai/tests/grinder.rs` (10). Finding (Q-1280, answered by S-0212 revision 49 at step 1i): a unit sent from afar gave up the order on the next tick when buildings stood across its route. This is not a grinder matter. `path::compute` follows S-0212 claims 13 and 13a: on open synthetic ground a straight line of seven 1 × 1 buildings, 20 cells away, can defeat a vehicle's or an infantry unit's move to a cell just behind it, and the recycle order to a grinder behind it. Two cell searches fail; the retries close level-0 and level-1 connections but none at level 2, because there the free part borders the next sector; then the new coarse search fails at level 1, and claim 13's table fails the computation. S-0212's short-line edge case confirmed this as the original's behaviour with `quirks.corridor_retry_no_fallback` on (the default); off, one more cell search without a corridor follows and the line is passed at every alignment (since S-0212 r82, intake 34, the buildings' exemption rectangles pass every such line at every shift, and the two steps stay for strips and ledges). Before r82, in the synthetic geometry of `run_s_shifts_with_the_switch_on`, lines of three and five passed at all eight shifts, the 7-line failed at shifts 0 and 1 and the 9-line at shift 0; since r82 every shift passes. The failed unit gives up (S-0603 claim 3, S-0212 claim 23 row 2): *guard* queued, which *eaten* does not hold back: it becomes current at the first start or late start at which the unit is ready (S-0215 claim 11; S-0603 revision 6, step 1j, answering Q-1157). Because a person's direct click still fails at the default at a strip or a ledge (since r82, not at a line of buildings), the AI keeps its two steps.
- **Client and lane.** The local lane scene `grinder` (`crates/app/tests/m7a_lane.rs`, screenshots `local-lane/m7a/grinder-*.png`): three infantry, an own vehicle and an enemy vehicle under control are clicked in (cursor *recycle* for all five). They are consumed after 98, 99, 104, 116 and 163 ticks, and the house is paid. AI lane (map 8, seed 7, Yuri house 1; counts in `local-lane/m7a/s0603/`): sides 0,2: Allied won at 20 112; sides 1,2: Soviet won at 13 305. After merging step 1g (seed 7): map 8 sides 0,2: Allied won at 16 779; sides 1,2: Soviet won at 13 305; map 2 sides 0,2: Allied won at 18 913; sides 1,2: Yuri won at 29 979. All four: 0 grinder entries, 0 units ground, no recycling building. (Before the merge, map 2 sides 1,2 built one at about 16 500 and ground 1 unit.) Why, traced with a temporary probe of `Brain::build_grinder`: the stock recycling building is offered (`side-audit 2 8 7` orders it at 6 915 in the sandbox), and from 4 200 every condition holds but the money. Until about 4 600 to 5 100 the building line is busy with plan buildings while the grinder is affordable. After that, credits stay below twice its cost whenever the line is free (map 2 sides 1,2: from 6 900 to the end of the game). On map 8 sides 0,2, by the time money came back the house had lost a prerequisite building. So credits and the plan's use of the line decide it. No rule of ours stops a build a person would make, and the AI was not changed for it. Outputs: `ai-sides-0-2.txt`, `ai-sides-1-2.txt`, `ai-sides-0-2-map2.txt`, `ai-sides-1-2-map2.txt` (money values removed). Replays and mid-game saves matched in both.
- **Gate.** The M7a duel's Alpha may build GRND (2 × 2, cost 400, no power; Alpha only), and starts with four HULKs north of its start (an unarmed vehicle of the fixture that no house can build), placed at health 51 of 256, which a person would recycle. The run counts grinder entry orders and units ground (`Effect::Consumed`), and saves and loads at the first tick a unit is bound and the first consumption. Seed 3 (`M7A_SEED`, unchanged from step 1g): 4 entry orders, 4 ground, every other counter at least 3 except sensor orders 2. Minimum / median over seeds 1 to 16, measured after merging step 1g:
  - berserk orders 9/19, units made berserk 27/47;
  - sensor orders 2/2, intent ticks 1 869/3 316;
  - reveal orders 6/11, reveals fired 6/11;
  - slave-miner buildings 3/5, slave ticks 1 274/7 193;
  - gattling stage rises 6/14, top-stage ticks 448/2 373;
  - takeover orders 3/14, takeovers 7/15;
  - gas clouds 26/147, gas hits 39/487;
  - absorber entry orders 6/15, infantry absorbed 5/8;
  - grinder entry orders 4/8, units ground 4/8.

  Without the hulks the grinder counters missed the margin (before step 1g: minimum 0, median 3): after the grinder stands (tick 6 200 to 7 400) Alpha seldom has an idle controlled vehicle, nothing at or below 30 % health, and no surplus infantry at home. Letting Beta build GRND as well was tried and dropped. With the hulks, 8 of the 16 games end before 12 000 ticks (Alpha wins seeds 1, 2, 7, 8, 9, 12, 14 and 15); seed 3 plays the full run. The AI duels' goldens moved with the AI game format and were regenerated after the merge. At step 1i (S-0212 revision 49 with `corridor_retry_no_fallback`, S-0605 revision 5's approach, S-0506 revision 10's flagged slot) the goldens were regenerated and no golden file changed: no gate game reaches the changed rows, and every figure above (seed 3 and the sweep) is the same.

## Simulation (M7a: Vehicle bunker)

S-0606 (revision 3, revisions 4 and 5 since step 1k, 6 and 7 since step 1l) is built in `glasnost_sim::structures::bunker`; the ledger is [docs/m7-claims/S-0606.md](../m7-claims/S-0606.md), the tests are `claims_s0606.rs`, four pairs in `pairs_m7a.rs` (a gattling in a bunker spins up as in the open; a controlled vehicle goes into its controller's bunker; a warp takes a bunkered vehicle out; a berserk weapon is refused at a bunkered vehicle and at its bunker), `a_temporal_shot_at_a_bunkered_vehicle_erases_its_bunker` (`claims_s0514_more.rs`), the audio test `bunker_walls_sounds` and, for the importer, `bunker_fields`, `bunker_wall_anims_are_converted`, `bunker_foundation_warning` and `bunker_depot_warning`. The state is in `StructState`: the bunker's vehicle (`bunker_occupant`), the vehicle's bunker (`bunkered_in`) and the bunker's stage of table S1 (`bunker_stage`: clearing, waiting, turning in, driving in, turning south, raising walls, occupied); the entrant's contact is the ordinary contact field, and the entry and exit tracks (T1, T2) are `Track.bunker` in `drive`. All of it is saved and hashed. Save format version 53 (54 since step 1j).

- **Who may go in (claims 1 to 3).** `bunker::eligible`: the vehicle's bunkerable flag (absent counts as on), a turret, a turret weapon, not hover or balloon, no parasite on it. `bunker::permits` is the permission table (rows P1 to P10, first match wins); `targeting` reads it, so the *enter* cursor and click follow it. `bunker::request` takes the contact; a second vehicle is turned away and a switched-off bunker refuses.
- **Entry (claims 4 to 8, F1 to F3, T1).** The docking point (F1) from the vehicle's quadrant; the arrival (`bunker::arrival`, from `drive` only: the vehicle stops, keeps the bunker as destination and stays in *enter*, the bunker queues *repair*; claim 5, revision 4); then S1, one row per *repair* processing of the bunker with a delay of 15 (`bunker::process_repair`): clearing (objects in the foundation are asked to scatter and waited for; the candidates are the infantry, vehicles, aircraft and buildings of each foundation cell's ground list; `quirks.bunker_clear_chosen_only`, default on, asks only each cell's chosen object, and `quirks.bunker_clear_far_point`, default on, measures F5's distance from 896 leptons per axis off the cell's top corner, off from its centre; both tested both ways), the turn in, the drive along T1 (every point driven, claim 21), the turn to 32768, the walls going up (`Effect::Bunkered`, the walls-up sound and the special-2 to special-4 animations). The occupied bunker blocks its foundation (`occupancy`); the vehicle keeps *guard*, cannot be moved, is left out of band selection, and the client drops it from the selection (`game/play.rs`). `bunker::clear_report` is claim 8's report (384 leptons, the run-away reply); no route reaches it yet.
- **Inside (claim 9).** The range bonus (`combat::range`), the rate of fire (`combat::fire`), the hit rows (`combat::damage`: a penetrating warhead at an occupied bunker leaves it unaffected, a non-penetrating one at the bunkered vehicle likewise), the threat and the redirection to the bunker (`combat::acquire`, `temporal::deliver`). A bunkered vehicle has no approach (`approach`) and refuses every non-empty destination (claim 18), and counts for `transport::tank_bunker`.
- **Release (claims 11 to 15, F4, T2).** The deploy click on a bunkered vehicle gives *unload* (S-0214 row VD5); every order ends a bunkered vehicle's contact while the link holds (claim 17), and an order to a vehicle still entering ends its contact, so the bunker returns to clearing and stops special-1 and special-2 if the walls were going up (claim 16). `bunker::process_unload` lowers the walls (walls-down sound and effects), drives T2 to facing 192, gives the nearby cell as destination (none found: the placeholder cell (0, 0)) and queues *move*, and ends every contact of the bunker; an ordinary type then chooses 450 ticks, a harvester or weeder, deploy-into or simple-deployer type goes on with that rule; the deploy-fire rule and passenger unloading are skipped (claim 12, revision 7, answering Q-1164; S-0415 claim 1). A released simple-deployer takes its first in-place step in the release processing, whatever its passenger capacity, and under `quirks.deploy_transition_discards_orders` its return to *guard* replaces the queued *move* (S-0517 revision 10 claim 1; `claim_12_released_simple_deployer_deploys_in_place`, `claim_12_released_simple_deployer_with_presentation`). A destroyed, sold or erased bunker lets the vehicle out (`bunker::let_out`, from `combat::death`, `econ::sell` and `temporal`); a destroyed, erased or warped vehicle is released in place (`bunker::release_in_place`; walls down after `Effect::Killed`, S-0312). An owner change of either side keeps the link (claim 15); other removals end it silently on both sides, and a surviving bunker returns to clearing with *guard* queued (`bunker::removing`, claim 19, our departure).
- **Seams filled.** Psychic (`psychic::firing_refused`, S-0610 claim 3: a berserk weapon is refused at a bunkered vehicle and at its bunker); warp (`superweapons::warp`, S-0513 claim 34: the block takes a bunkered vehicle out in place); mind (`mind::can_take` refuses a bunkered vehicle, S-0601 claim 3; a controlled vehicle may enter its controller's bunker); temporal (S-0514 claims 1 and 12: a shot at a bunkered vehicle erases its bunker); death (S-0312); sell (S-0408); range and fire (S-0301); approach (S-0215); transport (S-0507); parasite (claim 1's parasite row); the gattling (S-0609 claim 5: no exclusion or delay of the firing step inside).
- **Seams left (M7b).** S-0612 (the Psychic Dominator's sweep of a bunkered vehicle, `bunker::holds_through_owner_change`). Magnetic lift (S-0607) is built at step 3.8: a lift hit at a bunkered vehicle goes to its bunker, which is not seized. Release under a pulse is the spec's open question. The drain of a bunker building (S-0608) is built at M7b step 3.3 (`approach::draining`; the pairs `a_drained_bunker_refuses_fire_and_sale_but_no_blackout` and `a_drained_bunker_keeps_its_vehicle_firing`; its occupants keep their own rules, S-0608 claim 18, taken in at M7b step 1d).
- **Importer and audio.** Buildings `Bunker`, vehicles `Bunkerable`, `[AudioVisual] BunkerWallsUpSound` and `BunkerWallsDownSound` (played as `Sound::BunkerWallsUp` and `Sound::BunkerWallsDown`); the importer warns when a bunker type's foundation is not 2 × 2 (Edge cases) and when a type is both a bunker and a repair depot (claim 20).
- **Bunker and depot on one type (claim 20).** The bunker's permission rows (`depot::accepts`, `targeting`), the repeated request also refused by claim 3's eligibility check, the bunker's docking point (`depot::docking_point`), the depot's movement-arrival exchange (`bunker::arrival` skips a depot), and a *repair* processing that runs one S1 row and then the depot's processing on the state that row left, whose delay is returned and whose own request replaces S1's queued *guard* (revision 7, answering Q-1163; `claim_20_one_s1_row_then_the_depot`).
- **Requests.** Q-1300 to Q-1308 are answered by revision 4 (step 1k), Q-1163 and Q-1164 by revision 7 (step 1l); no interim is left.
- **Own AI.** `glasnost_ai::bunker` (AI game format 22); see *Own AI (M6c: special abilities)*.
- **Client and lane.** The local lane scene `bunker` (`crates/app/tests/m7a_lane.rs`, screenshots `local-lane/m7a/bunker-*.png`: before, driving in, occupied, firing, released): a vehicle clicked onto the install's tank bunker (cursor *enter*) reaches it and the bunker steps through S1: waiting at 149 ticks, turning in 164, driving in 179, turning south 194, raising walls 224, occupied 239, the body at facing 32768. An enemy vehicle placed four cells off is hit from inside 40 ticks later; the deploy click on the bunkered vehicle (cursor *deploy*) ends the link on the next tick, the bunker goes back to clearing, and the vehicle drives out beside it. The bunker's wall animations (special-1 to special-4, `Effect::SpecialAnims`) are not drawn: the client skips the effect, so the screenshots show the vehicle in the open pit. AI lane (map 8, seed 7, Yuri house 1; outputs `local-lane/m7a/s0606/ai-sides-0-2.txt` and `ai-sides-1-2.txt`): sides 0,2: Allied won at 17 384; the Yuri house built 2 tank bunkers, gave 1 entry order and 1 exit order, 1 vehicle was bunkered and let out with both standing. Sides 1,2: Soviet won at 21 750; 2 bunkers, 2 entry orders, 2 exit orders, 2 vehicles bunkered and let out. House 0 (Allied, then Soviet) built no tank bunker in either game. Replays and mid-game saves matched in both. The same numbers before and after merging step 1i (the gate minima and the goldens did not move either).
- **Gate.** Both sides of the M7a duel may build BUNK (2 × 2, cost 600, strength 1 500, no impassable rows); GUNR and Alpha's gattling GATV may go in. The run counts bunker build, entry and exit orders and vehicles bunkered, and saves and loads at the first tick a vehicle is bunkered. Minimum / median over seeds 1 to 16 as measured when the bunker was built (gate seed 3 plays all 12 000 ticks; seeds 4, 12, 15 and 16 end with Alpha's win at 10 643, 10 578, 11 615 and 11 468). Since step 1k only seed 4 ends early (at 11 250); the bunker minima are still 4, 3, 3 and 3, and the full table is in [step1k-m7a.md](../m7-claims/step1k-m7a.md):
  - berserk orders 15/27, units made berserk 30/49;
  - sensor orders 2/2 (the known exception), intent ticks 2 346/3 644;
  - reveal orders 8/12, reveals fired 8/12;
  - slave-miner buildings 3/5, slave ticks 1 321/6 764;
  - gattling stage rises 6/12, top-stage ticks 570/1 704;
  - takeover orders 5/17, takeovers 9/14;
  - gas clouds 34/87, gas hits 74/305;
  - absorber entry orders 5/19, infantry absorbed 4/8;
  - grinder entry orders 4/5, units ground 4/5;
  - bunker build orders 4/4, bunker entry orders 3/8, vehicles bunkered 3/7, bunker exit orders 3/7.

  Before the AI's join exit and early builds, the exit orders stayed at 0: bunkers came late and nothing called the vehicles out. The base defence also took vehicles on their way in as attackers. The goldens were regenerated.

## Simulation (M7a: S-0215 revisions 63 and 67)

S-0215 revision 63 (taken in at M7a step 1k, [step1k-m7a.md](../m7-claims/step1k-m7a.md)) widened claim 11's "moving" for a vehicle with the driving locomotor to a body still turning towards its given facing, for any reason. Revision 67 (step 1l, [step1l-m7a.md](../m7-claims/step1l-m7a.md), answering Q-1160 and Q-1161) replaces the rest of the driving test: such a vehicle is moving for readiness while its body still turns, or while all three of these hold: its locomotor moves it in S-0206's sense (a locomotor destination, or a track target whose x and y differ from its own), it has a heading point (it is on a track, S-0207 Terms) and its integer movement step *s* (S-0207 claim 2, `drive::progress` at the read) is above 0. `process::moving` calls `process::drive_moving` for a driving vehicle (by type, or temporarily, S-0417 claim 5) and keeps `occupancy::in_progress` for every other actor. A stored path, a retained destination or a turn-in-place mark no longer holds a driving vehicle back, and nor does the first, truncated-to-zero step of an accelerating vehicle; a vehicle between two tracks (S-0207 claim 12's two-track cap), blocked or waiting for a retry is ready. A body turn ends by tick number (S-0206 claim 1), so the first start of the tick in which it ends already sees it ended and processes the started behaviour in that tick (B47). The late start reads the same current predicate, not a remembered displacement. `process::ready` keeps its exceptions ahead of the moving test. Every start that asks for readiness goes through `process::ready`: the first per-tick start and the late start, and the harvest unload rows (S-0402 revision 31), the grinder's queued *guard* (S-0603 revision 8), the absorber's entrant, the depot's retry and arrival *sleep* (S-0314 revision 29), the release rows, mind control and the bunker. `occupancy::in_progress` itself is unchanged, so S-0206 claim 9's body turn and the scatter refusals keep S-0206's term. An attack order during a pre-step turn removes both destinations and the route; `drive::tick` then ends the turn in place at its next update, the body completes its turn and the cancelled step is not taken (S-0207 revision 39 claim 11; D11 c). S-0207 lets the old path steps survive; ours are cleared with the destination, which the claim allows. Tests: `claims_s0215_r63.rs` (a turn to face a target, the exceptions, ships and hovering vehicles stay ready, B47 with its same-tick processing) and `claims_s0215_r67.rs` (a retained goal and path alone are ready; a heading point with *s* above 0 is not, with *s* = 0 it is; B48's fresh move from *area guard*), `claim_11_queued_guard_starts_by_the_current_predicate` (`claims_s0610_r8.rs`).

- **Formats and gate.** No new state at either step: save format 54 and AI game format 22, unchanged. The goldens were regenerated at both steps (`duel`, `duel-m6c` and `duel-m7a` play differently). Step 1k: over seeds 1 to 16 every minimum is at least 3 except the sensor orders (2); seed 3 plays all 12 000 ticks, and seed 4 ends at 11 250 with Alpha's win. Step 1l: the gate seed 3 plays all 12 000 ticks with every minimum but the sensor orders at least 3, but over seeds 1 to 16 seed 8 gives no takeover orders and seed 9 no grinder entry orders or ground units (no setup-only fix found; left to the lead). `combat` also changes at step 1l. The tables are in the step 1k and step 1l ledgers.

## Simulation (M7a: Magnetic lift)

S-0607 (revision 18, taken in at M8 A3 intake 49, [intake49-S-0607.md](../m8-claims/intake49-S-0607.md): claim 11's no approach step against the gripped unit, in `approach::step`, with part h's approaches ending the grip, tests in `claims_s0607_r18.rs`; revision 14 at intake 45, [intake45-S-0607.md](../m8-claims/intake45-S-0607.md); revision 2 built at step 3.8, revision 3 taken in at step 1m, [step1m-m7a.md](../m7-claims/step1m-m7a.md), revision 4 at step 1n, [step1n-m7a.md](../m7-claims/step1n-m7a.md), revisions 5 and 6 at step 1o, [step1o-m7a.md](../m7-claims/step1o-m7a.md), revision 7 at step 1p, [step1p-m7a.md](../m7-claims/step1p-m7a.md), revision 8 at step 1q, [step1q-m7a.md](../m7-claims/step1q-m7a.md), revision 9 at step 1r, [step1r-m7a.md](../m7-claims/step1r-m7a.md)) is built in `glasnost_sim::lift`, with hooks in `jump` and `jump::fly`. The ledger is [docs/m7-claims/S-0607.md](../m7-claims/S-0607.md). Tests: `claims_s0607.rs` (45), `claims_s0607_standin.rs` (15), `claims_s0607_release.rs` (18), `claims_s0607_r7.rs` (19) and `claims_s0607_r18.rs` (7), fixtures in `lift_common/mod.rs`; five pairs in `pairs_m7a.rs` (a lifted controlled vehicle dies and frees its controller's slot; a lift attack on a bunkered vehicle goes to its bunker; a seized gattling spins down; a seized vehicle ignores its owner's recycle click; a drone stays on a seized host); the importer tests `lift_fields`, `lift_fields_absent`, `lift_locomotor_malformed_keeps_the_earlier_layer` and `lift_death_weapon_given`; the AI tests `crates/deterministic/ai/tests/lifter.rs`; the tally test `counts_seized_and_dropped_units`. The state is `Object.lift` (`LiftState`: seized, set down, dropped, the stand-in and the stashed own locomotor states, the grip kept with no destination `grip_without_dest`, the gripped unit, the lifter, the dropper and its house, the crash rate) and the magnetic waves (`World.waves`, keyed by their own object id: `Wave`, firer, target and fade). All of it is saved and hashed (save format 58).

- **The seize (claims 1 to 6, F1).** `combat::projectile` sends a hit whose warhead has the locomotor flag to `lift::seize_hit`. The hit does no damage. `lift::eligible` is claim 3 (a ground vehicle, not invulnerable, not in factory contact, not already seized, type size below the shot's damage, F1). A takeover warhead suppresses the seize (`mind`). A temporarily driving harvester ends its replacement or stops (claim 4). The seized unit stops, gives up its mark, starts *guard*, loses its spawned objects, releases a naval parasite, and gets the jumpjet locomotor with its own type's jumpjet fields. A second seize by the same lifter drops the first unit in the same tick. A house that is not human queues *hunt* (claim 6). Claim 3's conditions come before claim 4 (State and ordering). The warhead's locomotor decides the rest (claim 22, `combat::data::SeizeLocomotor`): the jumpjet one seizes as above; one that can stand in (drive, walk, ship, teleport, drop pod, and an absent key, which is the earlier layer's value, else teleport) seizes with that stand-in (claims 22 to 24, below); one that cannot (hover, mechanical walking, fly, rocket, tunnel, an unknown identifier) stops the target and puts it in *guard*, takes its spawned objects and parasite and releases the firer's previous victim, but does not seize it (`lift::not_seized`). Claim 4 comes first whatever the locomotor, an unknown identifier included (revision 5).
- **Other stand-ins (claims 22 to 24, revision 4).** `lift::install_stand_in` gives up the unit's marks as claim 5 does (`jump::give_up_marks` for a jumpjet type), stashes its own locomotor states in `LiftState.own` (`OwnLocomotor`) and installs a fresh driving state (drive, ship) or teleporting state (also for a landed aircraft, whose own teleport state is stashed; revision 7). Occupancy (`quirks.lift_standin_unmarked_cell`, default on; revision 7, Q-1338): installing it marks no cell, so the unit holds no mark until its stand-in's own movement marks one, keeps its last cell's mark after moving, and an unmoved unit stays unmarked for good and clears no mark it does not hold (`lift::stand_in_marks`); the teleporting landing cell is marked at the seize without releasing anything else (`teleport::fix_landing_with`); the drop pod's own locomotor marks the cell again. Off, driving, walking and ship keep their own cell's vehicle mark at the seize. The unit stays listed: while the lifter is its destination a walking or teleporting stand-in is between steps (`lift::stand_in_between_steps`, read by `drive::between_steps_or_chain`), so an allied mover meets *moving blocker*, and after the grip *stationary friendly*. The order-level destination becomes the lifter object, and the stand-in gets the lifter's point of claim 7 (`lift::instruct_stand_in`; for teleport `teleport::stand_in_instruction`). `tick` sends a unit with a moving stand-in to `lift::stand_in_tick` (drive, `naval::tick` for ship, `teleport::tick`; walk does nothing, design; a landed aircraft jumps with teleport and does nothing with drive, walk or ship, design, revision 7). `lift::current_locomotor` is the one answer to "which locomotor moves this unit now": readiness (`process::driving`, S-0215 claim 11 revision 68), crushing and the teleporter test read it. `lift::enforce`, after movement, at the start of the behaviour turn and in `econ::assign_destination`, keeps the destination on the lifter while gripped and ends the grip with the unit set down when a rule removes it (claim 12's no-destination row; for teleport that is the jump, whose target drop also clears the lifter's target, or the first movement update when the landing point is the own position, revision 7). A landing search with no cell at the seize keeps the grip with no destination (`LiftState.grip_without_dest`); a later empty assignment ends it (`lift::given_no_destination`). Drop pod ends at once (`lift::drop_pod_ends`: own marks back, grip ended, set down, still seized; the lifter's target is cleared, claim 12's drop-pod row, revision 7). Restoration: `quirks.lift_standin_stays_seized` (default on) never restores drive, walk, ship or teleport and leaves drop pod seized; off, claim 20 applies with the stand-in's own moving test (`lift::stand_in_moving`) and `lift::end_stand_in` puts the own states and marks back.
- **The pull (claims 7 to 11).** At the seize the movement point is fixed (`jump::lift_point`): S-0502 claim 16's search around the lifter's point (`lift::lifter_point`: a mobile lifter's heading point, a building's foundation centre, or its docking point when it has the pad, repair-depot or vehicle-bunker flag, revision 5: the obsolete-unloading fixed point first, then the resource-unloading point at the centre +128 x, then the bunker's quadrant point, then the docking places, with a held contact choosing among several). The order-level destination is the cell found. It is not refreshed; `lift::steer` only keeps the destination on it. Within 20 leptons the grip ends and the unit is dropped over that cell, normally a free cell beside the lifter, so it lands unhurt (claim 8). A seized unit ignores destinations (`lift::refuses_destination`, claim 9; also a recycle click, and the deployed or changing rules of `econ`). It does not fire (claim 10). Against its own gripped unit the lifter answers *rearming* with any weapon, right after the busy test and ahead of the layer, height and projectile tests (claim 11, `combat::check`); the magnetic beam's answer against other targets stays after them. Deploying, undeploying and unloading are not refused (claim 10).
- **The end of a grip (claims 12 to 14).** `lift::end_grip`, from every row of claim 12: arrival, an accepted destination (`lift::lifter_accepts`), a new hit, another seize, either side leaving play (`lift::removing`, from `objects`), no destination, a fall-back to rest (`lift::lifter_rest`, from `process::fall_back`), and the two building rows (`lift::building_turn`, `lift::building_destroyed`). Above 1 lepton the unit is dropped, otherwise set down (claim 13). The stop on dropping clears its destination, route and target, breaks off its contact and announces `Effect::Dropped` (the client deselects); then S-0502 claim 17's stop runs on its jumpjet movement, at a drop and at a set-down alike (revisions 5 and 6, `jump::lift_release_stop`): a found point is kept with the movement commitment, flight state, speeds and target height untouched; with no cell (lifter-ended, design) nothing self-destructs in either quirk position, both a dropped and a set-down unit lose their point and commitment (revision 8, Q-1342 answered); a dropped unit falls, with the direct reference over its cell centre (off it, the detour's point is its own cell's centre) and an update every tick, and a set-down one stops. Claim 9's no-destination exception takes S-0502 claim 17's ordinary outcome, quirk included. With the switch off and no cell (revision 8, design; Q-1343 answered), a claim 9 unit in the air keeps hovering over its current cell, set down and seized, with no point and no commitment, and neither descends nor flies (`lift::set_down_released`); one on the ground stays grounded and is restored as usual (S-0502 revision 16: `jump::stop_with` keeps a *grounded* jumpjet grounded). A kept point in another cell makes the fall use S-0502 claim 9's look-ahead at zero speed (revision 14's F3, `jump::height_step`); `jump::lift_fall` keeps the point and commitment. A set-down unit still moving takes off, flies to the point and lands. The dropper house gets the credit (claim 14).
- **The fall and the impact (claims 15 to 19, F2).** `jump::fly` takes the unit to its cell centre with a growing crash rate (`lift::rate`, starting afresh at each seize at the type's crash value narrowed to single precision; the drop comes before the growth) and a target height of 0 (claim 15). A fall that crosses a bridge deck lands on it. `lift::fall_ended` reads what lies underneath (claim 16): infantry are erased (quirk `lift_drop_erases_cell_tail`), a vehicle blocks, anything else blocks. Clear land: the unit descends and touches down unhurt (claim 17). Blocked water: it sinks (`lift::sunk`). Blocked land (every S-0209 outcome but clear, claim 17): the impact (claim 18) hurts the unit by the falling damage F2 (`FallingDamageMultiplier`, `CurrentStrengthDamage`) with the crushing warhead, detonates the death weapon (none: a plain area detonation, claim 18's design rule, with an importer warning), crashes a crashable type, and removes a survivor (quirk `lift_impact_removes_survivor`, credited once). A unit a detonation has killed gets the impact credit a second time (quirk `lift_impact_double_credit`, default on; `lift::impact_credit` credits from the snapshot when the unit is gone). A dropped aircraft detonates the demolition warhead first (claim 19). The ground layer is read even on a deck (quirk `lift_deck_impact_reads_ground`). All four quirks are tested both ways.
- **Restoration (claim 20).** A set-down seized unit whose jumpjet movement is not moving (revision 6: not while a movement commitment lasts) gets its own locomotor back. Revision 14 (answers Q-2035): the test is made at the end of each of its turns (`lift::end_of_turn`, last in `tick::object_turn`), whatever its behaviour and house, and still at a fall-back to rest (`lift::restore_at_rest`, from `process::fall_back`); both use `lift::restoration_test`. A clear-cell touchdown is restored at the end of the touchdown turn, *guard* and *hunt* alike; restoration starts no behaviour, so a hunter hunts from its next processing. Turns that end early have no test (lost, dead, sinking, being erased, warp-only updates); a unit being erased or in a chrono move's leaving stage is not restored at either test. Revisions 8 and 9 (design, `jumpjet_no_cell_self_destruct` off; Q-1344): a set-down unit hovering with no commitment, by any route, is not tested; at the end of each of its turns (revision 14; not at a fall-back to rest) it repeats the stop's search (`jump::lift_retry_stop`, S-0502 claim 17's search around its cell): with a cell it re-orders itself to the cell's centre (a movement point only), flies there, lands and is restored at the end of its landing turn; with none it keeps hovering. The check is the derived state (`jump::hovering_uncommitted`: set down, no lifter, *hovering*, no movement point, not moving), so it adds no saved field. At height 0 or less the stop leaves the unit *grounded* instead (`jump::stop_with`), and it is restored as usual. With the switch on claim 20's two original conditions decide. A seized survivor is put on the surface or the deck. Readiness asks the current locomotor (S-0215 claim 11, revision 68; `process::driving` reads `lift::current_locomotor`): a jumpjet-seized vehicle uses the jumpjet's *moving now* (claim 20), a driving stand-in the driving predicate; tests `claim_5_seized_turning_vehicle_is_ready`, `s0215_claim_11_current_locomotor`.
- **The magnetic wave (claim 21, F3, F4).** `combat::fire` calls `lift::shot`: no wave when the distance test F3 fails (a sum that wraps negative takes the root of its negation). The wave gets a new object id, is appended to the update order and updates once at creation; then `lift::wave_turn` runs in its own turn (`tick`'s object turn), fades it over 20 updates (F4) and removes it (claim 21). Its firer or target leaving play, which includes going inside a transport, bunker or building, ends it. A live wave counts for S-0301 claims 5 and 11.
- **Seams filled.** Jumpjet (`jump::seized` reads lift); in-place deployers (`inplace::beam_refused`, S-0517 claim 11); bunker (a lift hit at a bunkered vehicle goes to its bunker, which is not seized); mind control, gattling, parasites, spawners, naval sinking credit, temporal, superweapons, grinder, the factory release, cloak and approach take the seized state through their ordinary hooks.
- **Gaps.** The S-0309 claim 24 redirect to a bunker is applied by the target search only, not to an ordered target; `lift` redirects at the seize. The client does not draw the wave.
- **Importer.** Warheads `IsLocomotor` and `Locomotor` (a locomotor class identifier of S-0207 table T3, classed by claim 22; blanks trimmed; an unknown well-formed identifier warns and does not seize; a malformed value is ignored as if absent, keeping an earlier layer's value, and warns on a seizing warhead; an empty value is no entry under S-0001 claim 8 and does not warn, claim 22 revision 7), `[CombatDamage] FallingDamageMultiplier`, `CurrentStrengthDamage` and `DeathWeapon` (absent: a warning, claim 18).
- **Requests.** Q-1320 and Q-1322 to Q-1331 are answered by revision 3 (step 1m), Q-1321 by revision 4 (step 1n); their interim rules are retired. Revision 4 answers Q-1334 (claims 23 and 24) and Q-1335 (mechanical walking cannot stand in) in substance; both are built (the request files are left to the lead). Revisions 5 and 6 answer Q-1332 to Q-1336 (step 1o; Q-1334 and Q-1335 were already built) and Q-1338 in substance (claim 23's Occupancy bullet); their interims are retired. Revision 7 answers Q-1337 to Q-1341 (step 1p); revision 8 answers Q-1342 and Q-1343 (step 1q); revision 9 answers Q-1344 (step 1r); their interims are retired. S-0207 revision 41 (claim 20 b) and S-0209 revision 43 (claim 1) point at S-0607 claim 23 for the no-cell landing search and the stand-in marks; both are built through it. No request of the spec is open.
- **Own AI.** `glasnost_ai::lifter` (AI game format 23); see *Own AI (M6c: special abilities)*.
- **Client and lane.** (Recorded under revision 2; not re-run at step 1m, where the pull goes to a fixed cell beside the lifter.) The local lane scene `lift` (`crates/app/tests/m7a_lane.rs`, screenshots `local-lane/m7a/lift-before.png`, `lift-seized.png`, `lift-drop.png`, `lift-impact.png`) uses the install's lifter type and an unarmed liftable vehicle. The attack click shows the *attack* cursor. The vehicle is seized 14 ticks later, unhurt. After 201 ticks it is over a second vehicle at height 883. A move click on the lifter (cursor *move*) ends the grip 2 ticks later (dropped). The impact follows 43 ticks later: the dropped unit leaves play and the vehicle underneath is at 0 per mille. AI lane (map 8, seed 7, Yuri house 1; outputs `local-lane/m7a/s0607/ai-sides-0-2.txt` and `ai-sides-1-2.txt`): after merging step 1l: sides 0,2: Allied won at 18 682; the Yuri house built 2 lifters, gave 1 lift order and 1 drop order; 2 units were seized and 2 dropped. Sides 1,2: Soviet won at 32 468; 1 lifter, 1 lift order, no drop order, 4 seized, 4 dropped (lifters also fire at what they acquire themselves, and a dropped unit's grip may end by arrival or the lifter's death). Before step 1l: sides 0,2: Allied won at 16 081 (1 lifter, 1 lift order, 2 drop orders, 4 seized, 4 dropped); sides 1,2: Yuri won at 40 802 (2 lifters, 4 lift orders, 4 drop orders, 12 seized, 8 dropped). House 0 (Allied, then Soviet) cannot build a lifter. Replays and mid-game saves matched in both. Before the first lifter was queued behind the war factory's work, the Yuri house built none in either game.
- **Gate.** Both sides of the M7a duel may build MAGN (cost 400, strength 400, beam MAGRAY of damage 10, so every fixture vehicle of size 1 can be seized). With an Alpha-only lifter, Alpha won the gate seed before its last tick. The run counts lifter build orders, lift orders, vehicles seized and seized units dropped, and saves and loads at the first tick a unit is seized and the first tick one is dropped. Minimum / median / maximum over seeds 1 to 16, after merging step 1l (gate seed 3 plays all 12 000 ticks; seed 15 ends with Alpha's win at 11 968):
  - berserk orders 14/28/64, units made berserk 42/47/59;
  - sensor orders 2/2/2 (the known exception), intent ticks 2 296/4 701/5 667;
  - reveal orders 11/12/12, reveals fired 11/12/12;
  - slave-miner buildings 3/4/12, slave ticks 1 980/7 127/9 293;
  - gattling stage rises 6/17/31, top-stage ticks 282/2 797/3 758;
  - takeover orders 3/26/43, takeovers 11/21/26;
  - gas clouds 10/174/275, gas hits 17/735/1 433;
  - absorber entry orders 10/17/60, infantry absorbed 4/8/12;
  - grinder entry orders 5/11/21, units ground 5/9/12;
  - bunker build orders 4/4/4, bunker entry orders 3/7/13, **vehicles bunkered 2/5/7, bunker exit orders 2/5/6**;
  - lifter build orders 5/6/7, lift orders 6/13/25, vehicles seized 4/15/27, seized units dropped 4/11/17.

  **Unresolved:** bunker exit orders are 2 in seeds 4 and 13, and vehicles bunkered 2 in seed 13 (on main after step 1l, without lift: 3 and 3). Step 1l's shortfalls (takeover orders 0 in seed 8, takeover orders 1 and grinder entry orders 0 in seed 9) do not occur on this branch. After merging step 1k (before step 1l) the shortfall was elsewhere: gattling stage rises 2 and gas clouds 1 in seeds 11 and 14. A probe then found one GATV seized in each of those seeds, so the lifters were not dropping the gattlings; the course of the games had changed. Making GATV too heavy to lift (size 10) raised those counters, but Alpha then won the gate seed at 11 583, so the change was reverted. Each fixture or AI change moves which seeds fall short, so none was kept for the gate.

  **Step 1m (revision 3).** Seeds 1 to 16 after the step (minimum / median / maximum): berserk orders 14/33/43, units made berserk 38/45/75; sensor orders 2/2/2, intent ticks 2 956/4 128/5 488; reveal orders 8/12/12, reveals fired 8/12/12; slave-miner buildings 3/4/12, slave ticks 1 980/7 323/8 928; gattling stage rises 0/19/34, top-stage ticks 0/2 730/4 108; takeover orders 6/26/38, takeovers 13/19/27; gas clouds 0/152/301, gas hits 0/589/1 502; absorber entry orders 9/17/43, infantry absorbed 4/8/15; grinder entry orders 4/11/17, units ground 4/10/15; bunker build orders 4/4/4, bunker entry orders 4/7/11, vehicles bunkered 1/4/8, bunker exit orders 1/4/7; lifter build orders 5/5/7, lift orders 5/14/18, vehicles seized 4/12/17, seized units dropped 3/11/16. Shortfalls: seed 8 gattling stage rises 0, top-stage ticks 0, gas clouds 0 and gas hits 0; seed 13 vehicles bunkered 1 and bunker exit orders 1; seed 4 bunker exit orders 2. **The gate seed 3 meets every minimum (except the sensor's 2) but ends at tick 11 222 with Alpha's win**, so the gate test's full-length assertion fails. The likely cause is claim 8: arrival drops now land unhurt, so lifted vehicles survive. Seeds 6, 7, 12, 13 and 16 also end early with Alpha's win. The seed is left to the lead; seeds 1, 2, 5, 9, 10, 11, 14 and 15 play all 12 000 ticks with only the sensor exception.
